## 新投稿 (130)

<h3 id="1">[1] 2026 Roadmap on Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning for Smart Manufacturing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00839](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00839)
- **Authors**: Jay Lee, Hanqi Su, Marco Macchi, Adalberto Polenghi, Wei Wu, Zhiheng Zhao, George Q.Huang, Kiva Allgood, Devendra Jain, Benedikt Gieger, Vibhor Pandhare, Soumyabrata Bhattacharjee, Ram Mohril, Lingbao Kong, Qiyuan Wang, Xinlan Tang, Sungjong Kim, Chan Hee Park, Byeng D. Youn, Guo Dong Goh, Xi Huang, Wai Yee Yeong, Yung C Shin, He Zhang, Zitong Wang, Fei Tao, Jagjit Singh Srai, Satyandra K. Gupta, Byung Gun Joung, Albin John, John W. Sutherland, Sang Won Lee, Olga Fink, Vinay Sharma, Faez Ahmed, Wei Chen, Mark Fuge, Arild Waaler, Martin G. Skjæveland, Dimitris Kyritsis, Wei Chen, VispiNevile Karkaria, Yi-Ping Chen, Ying-Kuan Tsai, Joseph Cohen, Xun Huan, Jing Lin, Liangwei Zhang, Gregory W. Vogl, Aaron W. Cornelius, Xiaodong Jia, Dai-Yan Ji, Takanobu Minami, Ruoxin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Manufacturing AI, Survey
- **Venue**: Machine Learning: Engineering 2026
- **Summary**: 本文是一份关于人工智能和机器学习在智能制造中应用的发展路线图，涵盖了工业大数据分析、先进传感、自主系统、数字孪生、机器人技术、供应链优化等现有应用，以及物理信息AI、生成式AI、可解释AI、大语言模型等新兴方向。

<h3 id="2">[2] AI Agents for Sustainable SMEs: A Green ESG Assessment Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00841](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00841)
- **Authors**: Viet Trinh, Tan Nguyen, Minh-Huyen Phan, Quan Luu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; econ.GN
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Sustainability
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于AI代理的框架，用于评估欧洲中小企业的ESG（环境、社会和治理）绩效，该系统利用大语言模型实现自动化ESG分类和情境化建议生成，与人工评估结果高度一致。

<h3 id="3">[3] Understanding Emergent Misalignment via Feature Superposition Geometry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00842](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00842)
- **Authors**: Gouki Minegishi, Hiroki Furuta, Takeshi Kojima, Yusuke Iwasawa, Yutaka Matsuo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, AI Safety, Interpretability
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究了大语言模型中涌现性错位现象的机制，提出基于特征叠加几何的解释：由于特征以重叠表示编码，微调目标特征时会意外增强相邻的有害特征，并提出几何感知方法可将错位减少34.5%。

<h3 id="4">[4] ClinicBot: A Guideline-Grounded Clinical Chatbot with Prioritized Evidence RAG and Verifiable Citations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00846)
- **Authors**: Navapat Nananukul, Mayank Kejriwal
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: RAG, Medical AI, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了ClinicBot，一个基于指南的临床聊天机器人系统，通过结构化提取临床指南、证据优先级排序和可验证引用，提供准确、可验证且明确基于官方指南的临床诊断支持。

<h3 id="5">[5] Accelerating battery research with an AI interface between FINALES and Kadi4Mat</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00909](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00909)
- **Authors**: Giovanna Tosato, Leon Merker, Monika Vogler, Michael Selzer, Arnd Koeppe
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Bayesian Optimization, Active Learning, Battery Research
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种连接FINALES和Kadi4Mat研究数据管理生态系统的AI框架，利用主动学习和多目标贝叶斯优化来优化钠离子电池的形成协议，平衡形成时间与寿命性能。

<h3 id="6">[6] Effect-Transparent Governance for AI Workflow Architectures: Semantic Preservation, Expressive Minimality, and Decidability Boundaries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01030)
- **Authors**: Alan L. McCann
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO; cs.PL
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, AI Safety
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mashin-live/governance-proofs)
- **Summary**: 本文在Rocq中形式化了结构化治理的AI工作流架构，证明了效果级治理可以在不降低内部计算表达能力的情况下实施，建立了治理与计算表达能力的正交性。

<h3 id="7">[7] Algebraic Semantics of Governed Execution: Monoidal Categories, Effect Algebras, and Coterminous Boundaries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01032](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01032)
- **Authors**: Alan L. McCann
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO; cs.PL
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, AI Safety
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mashin-live/governance-proofs)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了治理执行的代数语义框架，基于交互树和参数化余归纳构建，证明了治理代数诱导对称幺半范畴，每个张量复合都保持治理属性。

<h3 id="8">[8] A Knowledge-Driven LLM-Based Decision-Support System for Explainable Defect Analysis and Mitigation Guidance in Laser Powder Bed Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01100)
- **Authors**: Basit Mahmud Shahriar, Md Habibor Rahman
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Manufacturing AI, Knowledge Graph, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个知识驱动的决策支持系统，将结构化缺陷知识与LLM推理相结合，为激光粉末床熔融制造提供可解释的缺陷诊断和缓解指导，并集成了多模态图像评估模块。

<h3 id="9">[9] Virtual Speech Therapist: A Clinician-in-the-Loop AI Speech Therapy Agent for Personalized and Supervised Therapy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01101](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01101)
- **Authors**: Shakeel Sheikh, Patrick Marmaroli, MD Sahidullah, Slim Ouni, Fabrice Hirsch, Goncalo Leal, Bjorn W Schuller
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Medical AI, Speech Processing
- **Summary**: 本文开发了虚拟言语治疗师（VST），一个基于AI代理的平台，整合深度学习口吃分类和多代理LLM推理，为口吃评估和个性化治疗规划提供支持，同时保持临床医生在环的范式。

<h3 id="10">[10] Towards Multi-Agent Autonomous Reasoning in Hydrodynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01102)
- **Authors**: Jinpai Zhao, Albert Cerrone, Joannes Westerink, Clint Dawson
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个用于流体力学的多代理系统原型，通过层执行图协调专业化代理，解决了单代理架构中上下文饱和的瓶颈问题，在37个查询测试中达到93.6%的事实精度。

<h3 id="11">[11] New Bounds for Zarankiewicz Numbers via Reinforced LLM Evolutionary Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01120)
- **Authors**: Jay Bhan, Nicole Nobili, Srinivasan Raghuraman, Patrick Langer
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; math.CO
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Evolutionary Computation
- **Summary**: 本文使用基于大语言模型的进化算法OpenEvolve，首次确定了三个Zarankiewicz数的精确值，并为另外41个数建立了新的下界，展示了LLM引导的进化搜索在数学研究中的潜力。

<h3 id="12">[12] PERSA: Reinforcement Learning for Professor-Style Personalized Feedback with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01123)
- **Authors**: Ravi Ranjan, Utkarsh Grover, Xiaomin Lin, Agoritsa Polyzou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RLHF, Education Technology, LLM Personalization
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PERSA框架，通过RLHF方法将LLM调整为生成符合教授风格的编程反馈，同时保持内容正确性，在参数高效微 tuning 的约束下实现了风格迁移与知识保持的平衡。

<h3 id="13">[13] Iterative Finetuning is Mostly Idempotent</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01130](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01130)
- **Authors**: Zephaniah Roe, Jack Sanderson, Dang Nguyen, Julian Huang, Todd Nief, Aryan Shrivastava, Chenhao Tan, Ari Holtzman
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Fine-Tuning, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 本文研究了模型在自身输出上训练时行为倾向是否会放大，发现在监督微调和合成文档微调设置中特征大多衰减或保持稳定，而在DPO设置中偏好自身输出时可能出现特征放大。

<h3 id="14">[14] To Use AI as Dice of Possibilities with Timing Computation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01134](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01134)
- **Authors**: Jia Li, Vipin Kumar, Rui Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文引入了基于动词的AI建模范式，定义了时机计算和可能性的概念，在3276名乳腺癌患者的纵向EHR数据上实现了临床显著患者轨迹的自动发现和反事实时机推断。

<h3 id="15">[15] A Low-Latency Fraud Detection Layer for Detecting Adversarial Interaction Patterns in LLM-Powered Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01143)
- **Authors**: Sheldon Yu, Yingcheng Sun, Hanqing Guo, Julian McAuley, Qianqian Tong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Agent, Fraud Detection
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种低延迟欺诈检测层，用于检测LLM代理中的对抗性交互模式，通过建模交互轨迹上的风险而非单提示判断，实现了比LLM检测器快9倍以上的实时部署。

<h3 id="16">[16] Position: Safety and Fairness in Agentic AI Depend on Interaction Topology, Not on Model Scale or Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01147)
- **Authors**: Tanav Singh Bajaj, Nikhil Singh, Karan Anand, Eishkaran Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, AI Safety, Fairness
- **Summary**: 本文作为立场论文指出，代理AI的安全性和公平性取决于交互拓扑而非模型规模或对齐，识别了三种拓扑驱动的病理：顺序不稳定性、信息级联和功能性崩溃。

<h3 id="17">[17] Arithmetic in the Wild: Llama uses Base-10 Addition to Reason About Cyclic Concepts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01148)

<h3 id="54">[54] NeuroState-Bench: A Human-Calibrated Benchmark for Commitment Integrity in LLM Agent Profiles</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Agent, Benchmark

<h3 id="55">[55] Sheaf-Theoretic Planning: A Categorical Foundation for Resilient Multi-Agent Autonomous Systems</h3>

<h3 id="56">[56] CyberAId: AI-Driven Cybersecurity for Financial Service Providers</h3>

<h3 id="57">[57] Disentangling Intent from Role: Adversarial Self-Play for Persona-Invariant Safety Alignment</h3>

<h3 id="59">[59] Moira: Language-driven Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Pair Trading</h3>

<h3 id="60">[60] 12 Angry AI Agents: Evaluating Multi-Agent LLM Decision-Making Through Cinematic Jury Deliberation</h3>

<h3 id="63">[63] Reliable AI Needs to Externalize Implicit Knowledge: A Human-AI Collaboration Perspective</h3>

<h3 id="66">[66] NORA: A Harness-Engineered Autonomous Research Agent for End-to-End Spatial Data Science</h3>

<h3 id="79">[79] PhysicianBench: Evaluating LLM Agents in Real-World EHR Environments</h3>

<h3 id="86">[86] Anon: Extrapolating Optimizer Adaptivity Across the Real Spectrum</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02317)
- **Authors**: Yiheng Zhang, Kaiyan Zhao, Shaowu Wu, Yiming Wang, Jiajun Wu, Leong Hou U, Steve Drew, Xiaoguang Niu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Anon优化器，具有连续可调的自适应能力，可以在SGD和Adam行为之间插值甚至外推，并引入增量延迟更新机制确保收敛性。

<h3 id="87">[87] Can Causal Discovery Algorithms Help in Generating Legal Arguments?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02318](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02318)
- **Authors**: Soham Wasmatkar, Subinay Adhikary, Rakshit Rohan, Shouvik Kumar Guha, Saptarshi Pyne, Kripabandhu Ghosh

<h3 id="88">[88] ANO: A Principled Approach to Robust Policy Optimization</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 本文提出了锚定邻域优化(ANO)，一种鲁棒的策略优化方法，通过动态异常值抑制机制解决了PPO的样本效率低和SPO的不稳定性问题。

<h3 id="89">[89] A Compound AI Agent for Conversational Grant Discovery</h3>

<h3 id="90">[90] Controllable and Verifiable Process Data Synthesis for Process Reward Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02395)
- **Authors**: Yinghui Chi, Lucien Wang

<h3 id="91">[91] HeavySkill: Heavy Thinking as the Inner Skill in Agentic Harness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02396](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02396)
- **Authors**: Jianing Wang, Linsen Guo, Zhengyu Chen, Qi Guo, Hongyu Zang, Wenjie Shi, Haoxiang Ma, Xiangyu Xi, Xiaoyu Li, Wei Wang, Xunliang Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文将重型思维视为智能体编排系统中的内在技能，展示了这种两阶段(并行推理后总结)管道优于传统Best-of-N策略，并可通过强化学习进一步扩展。

<h3 id="92">[92] The Compliance Trap: How Structural Constraints Degrade Frontier AI Metacognition Under Adversarial Pressure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02398](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02398)
- **Authors**: Rahul Kumar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI Safety, LLM Alignment
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rkstu/schema-compliance-trap)
- **Summary**: 本文评估了前沿AI模型在对抗压力下的元认知稳定性，发现强制合规指令会导致认知崩溃，而移除合规后缀可恢复性能。

<h3 id="93">[93] FitText: Evolving Agent Tool Ecologies via Memetic Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02411](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02411)
- **Authors**: Kyle Zheng, Han Zhang, Renliang Sun, Chenchen Ye, Wei Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, LLM Agent, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 本文提出了FitText，一个无需训练的框架，通过在智能体推理循环中嵌入模因检索进化搜索，实现动态的工具检索。

<h3 id="94">[94] The Model Knows, the Decoder Finds: Future Value Guided Particle Power Sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02427](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02427)
- **Authors**: Tu Nguyen, Rasul Tutunov, Xiaotong Ji, Matthieu Zimmer
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了辅助粒子幂采样(APPS)，一种块状粒子算法，通过未来价值引导的选择来近似序列级幂目标，改进无训练解码的准确率-运行时权衡。

<h3 id="95">[95] Measuring AI Reasoning: A Guide for Researchers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02442)
- **Authors**: Munachiso Samuel Nwadike, Zangir Iklassov, Kareem Ali, Rifo Genadi, Kentaro Inui
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文为研究人员提供了评估语言模型推理能力的指南，主张应通过中间推理轨迹的忠实性和有效性来评估推理，而非仅关注最终答案准确率。

<h3 id="96">[96] Position: How can Graphs Help Large Language Models?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02452](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02452)
- **Authors**: Xiyuan Wang, Yi Hu, Yanbo Wang, Chuan Shi, Muhan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Learning, LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Graph
- **Venue**: FCS 2026
- **Summary**: 本文从三个角度探讨图如何帮助大语言模型：提供最新知识源减少幻觉、基于图的提示技术增强推理能力、以及改进对结构化数据的理解。

<h3 id="97">[97] Shadow-Loom: Causal Reasoning over Graphical World Model of Narratives</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02475)
- **Authors**: David Wilmot
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Knowledge Representation, Narrative Understanding
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Shadow-Loom框架，将叙事转化为版本化的图形世界模型，并应用因果物理（基于Pearl的因果阶梯）和叙事物理（评估悬疑、戏剧性讽刺、紧张感和惊喜）来分析故事结构。大语言模型仅用于边界处理（提取、渲染和审计），而因果推理和反事实推理则在图结构上进行。

<h3 id="98">[98] Efficient Temporal Datalog Materialisation for Composite Event Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02488](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02488)
- **Authors**: Periklis Mantenoglou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DB; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Event Extraction, Knowledge Representation, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文将多种事件规范语言的实用片段映射到时序Datalog，并提出流式触发图来支持高效的复合事件识别。该方法为跨不同事件规范语言的统一复合事件识别机制提供了基础。

<h3 id="99">[99] GRAIL: A Deep-Granularity Hybrid Resonance Framework for Real-Time Agent Discovery via SLM-Enhanced Indexing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02489](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02489)
- **Authors**: Jinliang Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: GRAIL是一个面向多智能体协作的智能体发现框架，通过SLM增强预测、伪文档扩展和MaxSim共振机制，实现了亚400毫秒的发现延迟，同时保持了高准确率。该框架在大规模AgentTaxo-9K数据集上验证，相比LLM解析基线延迟降低79倍以上。

<h3 id="100">[100] DataClaw: A Process-Oriented Agent Benchmark for Exploratory Real-World Data Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02503](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02503)
- **Authors**: Qiaohong Zhang, Weihao Ye, Jialong Chen, Yi Luo, BoYuan Li, Bowen Deng, Zibin Zheng, Jianhao Lin, Wei-Shi Zheng, Chuan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Data Science Automation
- **Summary**: DataClaw是一个面向探索性真实世界数据分析的过程导向基准测试，包含约206万条真实记录和492个跨领域任务，每个任务都标注了中间里程碑以支持过程级评估。实验显示当前最先进的LLM在该基准上表现不佳，七个模型整体准确率低于50%。

<h3 id="101">[101] Improving Model Safety by Targeted Error Correction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02544](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02544)
- **Authors**: Abolfazl Mohammadi-Seif, Ricardo Baeza-Yates
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Safety Evaluation, Image Classification
- **Venue**: ICPR 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种双分类器GBDT流水线方法，用于区分常规类人错误和高风险非人类误分类，在动物品种分类、皮肤病变诊断和前列腺组织病理学三个领域验证了其有效性。该方法在几乎不增加推理延迟的情况下，成功减少了危险的非人类错误。

<h3 id="102">[102] Strategy-Aware Optimization Modeling with Reasoning LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02545](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02545)
- **Authors**: Ruiqing Zhao, Fengzhi Li, Yuan Zuo, Rui Liu, Yansong Liu, Yunfei Ma, Fanyu Meng, Junlan Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning, Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rachhhhing/SAGE)
- **Summary**: SAGE是一个策略感知的优化建模框架，通过显式建模策略来改进LLM的优化程序生成。该方法构建了求解器验证的多策略数据集，并使用监督微调和分段加权GRPO进行训练，在八个基准测试上将平均pass@1从72.7提升到80.3。

<h3 id="103">[103] Double Rectified Linear Unit-based Modular Semantics for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02551](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02551)
- **Authors**: Gianvincenzo Alfano, Sergio Greco, Lucio La Cava, Francesco Parisi, Irina Trubitsyna
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Computational Argumentation, Knowledge Representation
- **Summary**: 该论文为定量双极论证框架(QBAFs)提出了基于双修正线性单元的新型渐进语义，能够产生更符合直觉的结果，同时满足文献中的理性公理。研究还证明了该方法不仅对无环QBAFs收敛，对更广泛的循环框架类也收敛。

<h3 id="104">[104] On Training Large Language Models for Long-Horizon Tasks: An Empirical Study of Horizon Length</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02572)
- **Authors**: Sunghwan Kim, Junhee Cho, Beong-woo Kwak, Taeyoon Kwon, Liang Wang, Nan Yang, Xingxing Zhang, Furu Wei, Jinyoung Yeo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Training, LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该研究系统性地考察了任务视野长度对LLM训练动态的影响，发现仅增加视野长度就会导致严重的训练不稳定性。研究表明视野缩减是解决这一问题的关键原则，在缩减视野下训练的模型能更有效地泛化到更长视野的任务。

<h3 id="105">[105] Universal Smoothness via Bernstein Polynomials: A Constructive Approximation Approach for Activation Functions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02591](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02591)
- **Authors**: Wentao Zhang, Yutong Zhang, Yifan Zhu, Wentao Mo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Activation Function, Image Classification, Deep Learning Theory
- **Venue**: ICIC 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种基于Bernstein多项式的通用平滑框架，并引入Bernstein线性单元作为新型激活函数。该方法通过构造可微的二次过渡区域消除奇点，同时保持分段线性结构，在图像分类基准测试上优于现有激活函数。

<h3 id="106">[106] Foundation-Model-Based Agents in Industrial Automation: Purposes, Capabilities, and Open Challenges</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02592](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02592)
- **Authors**: Vincent Henkel, Felix Gehlhoff, David Kube, Asaad Almutareb, Luis Cruz, Bernd Hellingrath, Philip Koch, Christoph Legat, Florian Mohr, Michael Oberle, Felix Ocker, Thorsten Schoeler, Mario Thron, Nico Andre Töpfer, Lucas Vogt, Yuchen Xia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Industrial Monitoring, Survey
- **Summary**: 该论文对工业自动化中基于基础模型的智能体系统进行了系统性文献综述，筛选了2341篇文献并综合分析了88篇。研究发现大多数系统处于原型和早期验证阶段，在人机交互方面表现突出，但在谈判能力方面存在明显不足。

<h3 id="107">[107] Counterfactual Reasoning in Automated Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02603](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02603)
- **Authors**: Alberto Pozanco, Daniel Borrajo, Manuela Veloso
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Planning, Causal Inference, Survey
- **Summary**: 该综述对自动规划中的反事实推理研究进行了分类，按照改变元素、触发时机、原因和方式进行归类，并讨论了关键发现和未来研究方向。

<h3 id="108">[108] SCGNN: Semantic Consistency enhanced Graph Neural Network Guided by Granular-ball Computing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02617](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02617)
- **Authors**: Genhao Tian, Taihua Xu, Shuyin Xia, Qinghua Zhang, Jie Yang, Jianjun Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Representation Learning, Semi-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: SCGNN是一个即插即用的图神经网络框架，利用粒球计算高效捕获语义一致性。该方法通过结构增强和监督增强双策略，将组级语义信息注入图结构并产生更可靠的监督信号，兼容多种GNN骨干网络。

<h3 id="109">[109] Trustworthy AI Suffers from Invariance Conflicts and Causality is The Solution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02640](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02640)
- **Authors**: Ruta Binkyte, Ivaxi Sheth, Zhijing Jin, Mohammad Havaei, Bernhard Schölkopf, Mario Fritz
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Fairness, Interpretability
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该立场论文认为可信AI目标（公平性、鲁棒性、隐私、可解释性）之间存在不变性冲突，而因果性是理解和平衡这些权衡的必要框架。论文将可信AI权衡重新解释为数据生成过程变化下的不兼容不变性要求，适用于经典ML模型和大规模基础模型。

<h3 id="110">[110] Deciphering Shortcut Learning from an Evolutionary Game Theory Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02658)
- **Authors**: Xiayang Li, Kuo Gai, Shihua Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Game Theory, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 该论文使用演化博弈论分析深度神经网络中捷径学习的形成机制，将数据样本建模为玩家、神经切线特征建模为策略。研究发现梯度下降和随机梯度下降导致不同的随机稳定状态，揭示了数据噪声和优化噪声对捷径偏见形成的影响。

<h3 id="111">[111] AcademiClaw: When Students Set Challenges for AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02661](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02661)
- **Authors**: Junjie Yu, Pengrui Lu, Weiye Si, Hongliang Lu, Jiabao Wu, Kaiwen Tao, Kun Wang, Lingyu Yang, Qiran Zhang, Xiuting Guo, Xuanyu Wang, Yang Wang, Yanjie Wang, Yi Yang, Zijian Hu, Ziyi Yang, Zonghan Zhou, Binghao Qiang, Borui Zhang, Chenning Li, Enchang Zhang, Feifan Chen, Feng Jian, Fengyin Sun, Hao Qiu, Hao Zheng, Haoran Zhu, Hongyu Liu, Jianbin Deng, Jiaxin Song, Jiaying Chi, Jiayou Shi, Jie Fang, Jinghui Zhong, Jingyu Zhou, Jinze Li, Junfeng Yi, Junyan Yu, Junzhi Xue, Ni Song, Pengyi Chen, Qi Chen, Quansheng Li, Rui Tao, Shenghai Gong, Shenhang Lu, Tianqi Shen, Tianxiang Zhu, Tiehan Kang, Tingyu Li, Wendi Wu, Xiao Shen, Xiao Zhou, Xiaotao Zhang, Xinrong Li, Xuankun Yang, Xun Zhang, Yan Li, Ye Lu, Yi Wang, Yibo Zhou, Yichi Zhang, Yihao Sun, Yijun Huang, Yixin Zhu, Yixuan Wu, Yuchen Sun, Yue Wu, Yuheng Sun, Yukun Li, Yutian Tu, Yuxuan Qin, Yuzhuo Wu, Zeyu Li, Zhengyu Lou, Zhenning Ran, Zizhu He, Pengfei Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/GAIR-NLP/AcademiClaw)
- **Summary**: AcademiClaw是一个双语基准测试，包含80个来自大学生真实学术工作流程的复杂长视野任务，涵盖25+专业领域。实验显示最先进的前沿模型仅能达到55%的通过率，揭示了当前AI智能体在学术级任务上的能力边界。

<h3 id="112">[112] An explainable hypothesis-driven approach to Drug-Induced Liver Injury with HADES</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02669](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02669)
- **Authors**: Maciej Wisniewski, Bartosz Topolski, Pawel Dabrowski-Tumanski, Dariusz Plewczynski, Tomasz Jetka
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Explainable AI, Medical AI
- **Summary**: HADES是一个用于药物性肝损伤(DILI)预测的智能体系统，将预测问题转化为可解释的假设生成问题。该系统结合分子级预测、代谢物分解、结构理解和毒性通路证据，在DILER基准测试上优于现有模型，同时建立了机制假设生成的基线。

<h3 id="113">[113] The 2026 ACII Dyadic Conversations (DaiKon) Workshop & Challenge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02672](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02672)
- **Authors**: Panagiotis Tzirakis, Alice Baird, Jeffrey Brooks, Emilia Parada-Cabaleiro, Lukas Stappen, Sharath Rao, Theo Lebryk, Jakub Piotr Clapa, Jens Madsen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Dialogue System, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了一个用于建模双人对话中人际情感和社会动态的基准测试，包含三个子任务：方向性人际影响预测、话轮转换预测和融洽关系轨迹预测。该基准基于包含945段双人对话的Hume-DaiKon数据集，支持多模态建模、时序推理和跨语境泛化。

<h3 id="114">[114] Hybrid Inspection and Task-Based Access Control in Zero-Trust Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02682)
- **Authors**: Majed El Helou, Benjamin Ryder, Chiara Troiani, Jean Diaconu, Hervé Muyal, Marcelo Yannuzzi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Access Control
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种混合运行时执行模型，结合确定性控制和语义检查，用于LLM驱动的智能体在多轮对话中的授权安全。该方法通过任务提取和任务-工具语义匹配两阶段机制，解决了现有委托授权流程缺乏对原始意图可见性的问题。

<h3 id="115">[115] An Empirical Study of Agent Skills for Healthcare: Practice, Gaps, and Governance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02709](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02709)
- **Authors**: Gelei Xu, Ningzhi Tang, Xueyang Li, Toby Jia-Jun Li, Zhi Zheng, Wei Jin, Yiyu Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Medical AI, Healthcare Monitoring
- **Summary**: 本文首次对医疗智能体技能进行了实证分析，通过对557个医疗相关技能的标注发现：公共医疗技能侧重于面向患者的工作流自动化而非诊断治疗任务，医疗生命周期覆盖不均，且通用技术风险无法可靠捕捉临床风险。

<h3 id="116">[116] ORPilot: A Production-Oriented Agentic LLM-for-OR Tool for Optimization Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02728](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02728)
- **Authors**: Guangrui Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Optimization, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 本文提出了ORPilot，一个开源的智能体AI系统，可将现实商业问题转化为求解器就绪的优化模型。该系统包含对话访谈智能体、数据收集智能体、参数计算智能体和求解器无关的中间表示，首次针对生产级商业问题而非教科书案例。

<h3 id="117">[117] Coherent Hierarchical Multi-Label Learning to Defer for Medical Imaging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02734](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02734)
- **Authors**: Joshua Strong, Pramit Saha, Emma Sun, Helen Higham, Alison Noble
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Medical AI

<h3 id="118">[118] AI and Open-data Driven Scalable Solar Power Profiling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02738)
- **Authors**: Shiliang Zhang, Sabita Maharjan, Damla Turgut
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Remote Sensing, Energy Management

<h3 id="119">[119] Foundation Models to Unlock Real-World Evidence from Nationwide Medical Claims</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文介绍了OceanPile，一个面向海洋基础模型的大规模多模态语料库，包含OceanCorpus、OceanInstruction和OceanBenchmark三个核心组件。该数据集整合了声纳数据、水下图像和科学文本，并通过多阶段质量控制确保科学有效性。

<h3 id="149">[149] Adversarial Flow Matching for Imperceptible Attacks on End-to-End Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00880) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Zeng, Xiangkun He, Lei Tao, Chen Lv, Hong Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Adversarial Robustness, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了对抗流匹配（AFM），一种针对端到端自动驾驶模型中Transformer结构漏洞的灰盒攻击框架。AFM能够高效生成视觉上不可感知的对抗样本，在保持攻击效果的同时具有强跨模型迁移性。

<h3 id="150">[150] Towards High Fidelity Face Swapping: A Comprehensive Survey and New Benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00883](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00883) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qi Li, Weining Wang, Shuangjun Du, Bo Peng, Jing Dong, Kun Wang, Zhenan Sun, Ming-Hsuan Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Image Generation, Survey
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CASIA-NLPRAI/face-swapping-survey)
- **Summary**: 本文对换脸方法进行了全面综述，将其组织为五种主要范式，并引入CASIA FaceSwapping基准数据集和标准化评估协议。该工作为开发更鲁棒、可控的换脸方法提供了统一视角和原则性评估框架。

<h3 id="151">[151] Selective Correlation Based Knowledge Distillation for Ground Reaction Force Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00888) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eun Som Jeon, Jisoo Lee, Huisu Lim, Omik M. Save, Hyunglae Lee, Pavan Turaga
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.IV; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Healthcare Monitoring, Time Series Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文提出了选择性相关知识蒸馏方法（SCKD），用于从可穿戴鞋垫传感器数据估计地面反作用力，解决了噪声干扰和计算资源限制问题。该方法利用时间特征进行知识迁移，生成的紧凑模型在实时分析中表现优异。

<h3 id="152">[152] Skeleton-Based Posture Classification to Promote Safer Walker-Assisted Gait in Older Adults</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00890](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00890) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sergio D. Sierra M., Monica Sinha, Marcela Múnera, Carlos A. Cifuentes
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Healthcare Monitoring, Human Activity Recognition, Pose Estimation
- **Summary**: 本研究评估了多种模型（几何方法、XGBoost、SVM和深度学习架构）在智能助行器使用分类、站立与坐姿识别以及姿态分类任务中的表现。结果表明XGBoost和几何方法在二分类任务中达到近完美准确率。

<h3 id="153">[153] X2SAM: Any Segmentation in Images and Videos</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00891) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao Wang, Limeng Qiao, Chi Zhang, Lin Ma, Guanglu Wan, Xiangyuan Lan, Xiaodan Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Segmentation, Vision-Language Model, Video Understanding
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了X2SAM，一个统一的分割多模态大语言模型，通过掩码记忆模块将任意分割能力从图像扩展到视频。该模型支持通用、开放词汇、指代表达、推理、交互式等多种分割任务，在图像和视频输入上均表现优异。

<h3 id="154">[154] Retrieval-Guided Generation for Safer Histopathology Image Captioning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00893](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00893) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md. Enamul Hoq, Wataru Uegami, Saghir Alfasly, Ghazal Alabtah, Sahar Rahimi Malakshan, Armita Kazemi, Alex T. Schmitgen, Fred Prior, H.R. Tizhoosh
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Captioning, RAG
- **Summary**: 本文研究了检索引导生成（RGG）作为组织病理学图像标注的更安全替代方案，通过总结视觉相似案例的专家文本生成标注。RGG在语义对齐方面优于完全生成式方法，余弦相似度达到约0.60，同时减少了不支持的诊断。

<h3 id="155">[155] Transfer Learning for Tonal Noise Prediction in VRF Units Using Thermodynamic and Vibration Signals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00895](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00895) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: ZhiWei Su, Ding Wang, Yuan Guo, Yang Qiao, HongJun Cao
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Transfer Learning, Predictive Maintenance, Time Series Forecasting
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于域不变偏最小二乘（Di-PLS）的无监督迁移学习方法，用于预测VRF室外机组的二次谐波噪声水平。基于加速度信号的Di-PLS模型表现最佳，预测误差保持在3 dB以内，揭示了振动信号与声辐射具有更强的因果关联。

<h3 id="156">[156] When Less Is More: Simplicity Beats Complexity for Physics-Constrained InSAR Phase Unwrapping</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00896](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00896) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prabhjot Singh, Manmeet Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Image Reconstruction, Remote Sensing, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026 Workshop
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/prabhjotschugh/When-Less-is-More-InSAR-Phase-Unwrapping)
- **Summary**: 本文挑战了在InSAR相位解缠中采用复杂注意力架构的趋势，证明普通U-Net在R²上优于注意力模型34%，在RMSE上优51%。研究揭示注意力机制会注入违反弹性表面变形平滑性约束的非物理高频伪影。

<h3 id="157">[157] RA-CMF: Region-Adaptive Conditional MeanFlow for CT Image Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00901](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00901) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Shifatul Ahsan Apurba, Md Selim, Jin Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Reconstruction, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文开发了一种条件均值流管道用于CT图像重建，将基于流的增强与强化学习驱动的空间控制相结合。该方法在肿瘤感兴趣区域实现了高精度，影像组学特征平均CCC为0.96，同时提升了整体图像质量。

<h3 id="158">[158] DIAGRAMS: A Review Framework for Reasoning-Level Attribution in Diagram QA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00905](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00905) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anirudh Iyengar Kaniyar Narayana Iyengar, Tampu Ravi Kumar, Manan Suri, Raviteja Bommireddy, Dinesh Manocha, Puneet Mathur, Vivek Gupta
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Document Understanding, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了DIAGRAMS，一个轻量级、模式驱动的图表问答推理级归因审查框架，将接口逻辑与数据集特定结构解耦。该框架在证据选择上达到85.39%的精确率和75.30%的召回率，有效减少了人工标注工作量。

<h3 id="159">[159] Generalized Category Discovery under Domain Shifts: From Vision to Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00906](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00906) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongjun Wang, Po Hu, Kai Han
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Vision-Language Model, Open-Vocabulary Segmentation
- **Summary**: 本文研究了域偏移下的广义类别发现问题，提出了三种框架（HiLo、HLPrompt、VLPrompt）来适应从自监督视觉模型到视觉语言模型的基础模型。这些方法通过多级特征提取、提示调优和跨模态一致性正则化来解耦域特征和语义特征。

<h3 id="160">[160] TRIP-Evaluate: An Open Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Large Models in Transportation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00907) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Han Gong, Zhen Zhou, Yunyang Shi, Yan Tan, Jinbiao Huo, Qi Hong, Zhiyuan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Autonomous Driving, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了TRIP-Evaluate，一个面向交通领域大模型评估的开放多模态基准，包含837个项目，覆盖车辆、交通管理、出行者和规划设计功能。评估结果揭示了模型在多步工程计算、规则约束推理和多模态场景理解方面存在显著弱点。

<h3 id="161">[161] Leveraging Imperfect Medical Data: A Manifold-Consistent Spatio-Temporal Network for Sensor-based Human Activity Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00913](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00913) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiangtao Fan, Anish Jindal, Amir Atapour-Abarghouei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human Activity Recognition, Time Series Analysis, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了MCSTN，一种流形一致时空网络，用于在传感器数据不完整或有噪声的情况下进行鲁棒的人体活动识别。该方法通过双层次损坏建模和双流时空架构学习稳定且抗损坏的语义表示，在三个基准数据集上取得了竞争性性能。

<h3 id="162">[162] The Cost of Consensus: Isolated Self-Correction Prevails Over Unguided Homogeneous Multi-Agent Debate</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00914](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00914) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Blaž Bertalanič, Carolina Fortuna
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Reasoning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该研究通过控制实验发现，同构多智能体辩论往往因从众效应、上下文脆弱性和共识崩溃而失败，且消耗更多token却无法获得更好的准确性。研究表明，对于7-8B参数级别的模型，隔离式自我纠正比无引导的同构辩论更具成本效益。

<h3 id="163">[163] Rethink MAE with Linear Time-Invariant Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00915](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00915) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zice Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision Transformer, Self-Supervised Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文挑战了视觉模型表示探测中将token视为无序词袋的传统范式，提出了基于状态空间模型的SSMProbe框架。研究发现token顺序是冻结视觉表示中的关键维度，不同预训练目标会形成不同的token结构特性。

<h3 id="164">[164] To Vibe Research or Not to Vibe Research? Generative AI in Qualitative Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00922](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00922) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Katja Karhu, Kari Smolander, Jussi Kasurinen
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Research Methodology, AI Ethics
- **Venue**: VibeX 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 该论文总结了生成式AI在定性研究中的争议性讨论，探讨了研究者是否应在定性研究中使用AI的问题。研究指出，定性研究方法、研究哲学、技能、伦理和个人偏好都是影响决策的重要因素。

<h3 id="165">[165] StyleShield: Exposing the Fragility of AIGC Detectors through Continuous Controllable Style Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00924](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00924) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guantian Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Text Detection, Adversarial Robustness, Text Style Transfer
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了StyleShield，首个用于条件文本风格迁移的流匹配框架，通过在连续token嵌入空间中操作来暴露AIGC检测器的脆弱性。该方法在中文多领域基准测试中实现了94.6%的规避率，同时保持0.928的语义相似度。

<h3 id="166">[166] PhaseNet++: Phase-Aware Frequency-Domain Anomaly Detection for Industrial Control Systems via Phase Coherence Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00929](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00929) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raviteja Bommireddy, Varshith Bandaru, Lohith Pakala, Pradeep Kumar B
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Industrial Monitoring, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了PhaseNet++，一种用于工业控制系统异常检测的频域自编码器，通过保留短时傅里叶变换的幅度和相位谱来利用之前被忽视的相位信息。该方法引入相位一致性指数构建传感器关系图，在SWaT基准上达到90.98%的F1分数。

<h3 id="167">[167] CellxPert: Inference-Time MCMC Steering of a Multi-Omics Single-Cell Foundation Model for In-Silico Perturbation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00930) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andac Demir, Erik W. Anderson, Jeremy L. Jenkins, Srayanta Mukherjee
- **Subjects**: q-bio.GN; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Bioinformatics, Single-cell Analysis
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CellxPert，一个将单细胞和空间多组学统一在共同表示空间的多模态基础模型。该模型支持细胞类型注释、高效微调、基因扰动响应预测和多组学整合四项任务，采用Metropolis-Hastings采样器生成生物学可解释的扰动轨迹。

<h3 id="168">[168] Code World Model Preparedness Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00932) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniel Song, Peter Ney, Cristina Menghini, Faizan Ahmad, Aidan Boyd, Nathaniel Li, Ziwen Han, Jean-Christophe Testud, Saisuke Okabayashi, Maeve Ryan, Jinpeng Miao, Hamza Kwisaba, Felix Binder, Spencer Whitman, Jim Gust, Esteban Arcaute, Dhaval Kapil, Jacob Kahn, Ayaz Minhas, Tristan Goodman, Lauren Deason, Alexander Vaughan, Shengjia Zhao, Summer Yue
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, AI Safety, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该报告记录了Meta的Code World Model代码生成和推理模型的准备情况评估，测试了其在潜在灾难性风险领域和不对齐倾向方面的表现。评估发现该模型不会带来当前AI生态系统之外的额外前沿风险，因此作为开放权重模型发布。

<h3 id="169">[169] CGM-JEPA: Learning Consistent Continuous Glucose Monitor Representations via Predictive Self-Supervised Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00933](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00933) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hada Melino Muhammad, Zechen Li, Flora Salim, Ahmed A. Metwally
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Medical AI, Time Series Analysis
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/CGM-JEPA)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CGM-JEPA，一个自监督预训练框架，通过预测掩码潜在表示而非原始值来学习跨模态可迁移的连续血糖监测表示。该方法在队列泛化和静脉到CGM迁移任务中分别比最强基线提升6.5和3.6个百分点。

<h3 id="170">[170] EventADL: Open-Box Anomaly Detection and Localization Framework for Events in Cloud-Based Service Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00936](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00936) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luan Pham, Victor Nicolet, Joey Dodds, Hui Guan, Daniel Kroening
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Cloud Computing, Fault Diagnosis
- **Venue**: FSE 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了EventADL，首个用于云服务系统的事件驱动异常检测与定位框架，通过学习事件语义模式和频率模式识别异常，并构建干预图进行根因定位。该方法在真实云服务系统上实现了至少90%的F1分数和100%的top-3根因定位准确率。

<h3 id="171">[171] Fusing Urban Structure and Semantics: A Conditional Diffusion Model for Cross-City OD Matrix Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00938) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bin Chen, Zhuoya Meng, Fang Yang, Runkang Guo, Jingtao Ding, Yin Zhang, Chuan Ai, Zhengqiu Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Graph Neural Network, Urban Mobility
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了SEDAN，一种基于结构增强扩散模型的跨城市OD矩阵生成方法，将城市建模为属性图并融合语义信息和空间约束。该方法通过图变换器建模潜在出行需求，在美国城市真实OD数据集上比最先进基线提升7.38%的RMSE。

<h3 id="172">[172] From Flat Facts to Sharp Hallucinations: Detecting Stubborn Errors via Gradient Sensitivity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00939](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00939) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yee Zhing Liew, Andrew Huey Ping Tan, Anwar P.P Abdul Majeed
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了嵌入扰动梯度敏感性方法，用于检测LLM中模型自信但错误的顽固幻觉。该方法基于鲁棒事实位于平坦极小值、顽固幻觉位于尖锐极小值的假设，通过扰动输入嵌入并测量梯度幅度变化来检测高置信度事实错误。

<h3 id="173">[173] Interpretable experiential learning based on state history and global feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00940) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anton Kolonin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Interpretability, Game AI
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种基于状态历史和全局反馈的可解释经验学习模型，能够学习由状态集之间的转移图表示的行为模型。该模型在Atari Breakout基准测试上展示了与神经网络方法相当的性能，适用于资源受限环境中的强化学习问题。

<h3 id="174">[174] SCARV: Structure-Constrained Aggregation for Stable Sample Ranking in Redundant NLP Datasets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00944](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00944) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xu Zheng, Feiyu Wu, Linhong Wu, Zhuocheng Wang, Hui Li
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Data Selection, Natural Language Understanding
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了SCARV，一个模块化聚合框架，用于在存在冗余结构的NLP数据集中实现稳定的样本级排名。该方法结合多种子鲁棒聚合和结构感知的冗余聚类聚合步骤，显著提高了排名的全局和局部稳定性。

<h3 id="175">[175] Co-Generative De Novo Functional Protein Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00948](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00948) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinrui Chen, Yizhen Luo, Siqi Fan, Zaiqing Nie
- **Subjects**: q-bio.QM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Protein Engineering, Generative Model, Bioinformatics
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CodeFP，一种用于从头功能蛋白质设计的共生成蛋白质语言模型，同时解码序列和结构token以实现功能性和可折叠性的双重优化。该方法在功能一致性和可折叠性上分别比最强基线提升6.1%和3.2%。

<h3 id="176">[176] Graph Rewiring in GNNs to Mitigate Over-Squashing and Over-Smoothing: A Survey</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00951](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00951) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hugo Attali, Nathalie Pernelle, Davide Buscaldi, Fragkiskos D. Malliaros
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Survey
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 该综述全面回顾了图神经网络中的图重连技术，这些技术旨在通过修改图拓扑结构来增强信息传播，从而缓解过度压缩和过度平滑问题。文章深入探讨了最先进重连方法的理论基础、实际实现和性能权衡。

<h3 id="177">[177] E-MIA: Exam-Style Black-Box Membership Inference Attacks against RAG Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00955) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zelin Guan, Shengda Zhuo, Zeyan Li, Jinchun He, Wangjie Qiu, Zhiming Zheng, Shuqiang Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Privacy, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文提出E-MIA，一种针对RAG系统的黑盒成员推断攻击方法，通过将目标文档中的可验证证据转换为考试形式的问题，利用聚合考试分数作为成员信号来判断文档是否被纳入知识库。

<h3 id="178">[178] "I Don't Know" -- Towards Appropriate Trust with Certainty-Aware Retrieval Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00957](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00957) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daan Di Scala, Maaike de Boer, Pınar Yolum
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: VALE 2025

- **Summary**: 本文提出了ProMORNA框架，利用多目标强化学习直接从目标蛋白序列设计全长mRNA转录本。该方法通过优化稳定性和翻译效率等生物学目标，在萤火虫荧光素酶案例研究中展现了优于基线的性能。

<h3 id="245">[245] Mesh Based Simulations with Spatial and Temporal awareness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01542](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01542) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="246">[246] 6G Needs Agents: Toward Agentic AI-Native Networks for Autonomous Intelligence</h3>

<h3 id="247">[247] Automated Interpretability and Feature Discovery in Language Models with Agents</h3>

<h3 id="248">[248] Neuro-Symbolic Agents for Hallucination-Free Requirements Reuse</h3>

<h3 id="249">[249] Model Merging: Foundations and Algorithms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01580](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01580) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="250">[250] KG-First, LLM-Fallback: A Hybrid Microservice for Grounded Skill Search and Explanation</h3>

<h3 id="251">[251] Where Do Prompt Perturbations Break Generation? A Segment-Level View of Robustness in LoRA-Tuned Language Models</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文提出了S2R2框架，通过将生成内容分解为语义片段并利用最优传输对齐，解决了现有鲁棒性方法在片段级语义漂移上的不足。实验表明，该方法在保持清洁性能的同时，显著提升了模型对提示扰动的鲁棒性。

<h3 id="252">[252] Concepts Whisper While Syntax Shouts: Spectral Anti-Concentration and the Dual Geometry of Transformer Representations</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文研究了Transformer表示中的双重几何现象，发现语义概念倾向于集中在频谱尾部，而语法信息则集中在高方差方向。这一发现为理解模型如何处理语义和语法提供了新的视角。

<h3 id="253">[253] Less Interaction But More Explanation: A Communication Perspective on Agentic AI Interfaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01610) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文从传播学视角探讨了智能体AI接口，指出随着AI自主性的增加，用户需要的常规交互减少，但对监督和解释的需求增加。文章提出了三种类型的解释，以在AI自主性增强时保持人类的主体地位。

<h3 id="254">[254] The Case for ESM3 as a General-Purpose AI Model with Systemic Risk Under the EU AI Act</h3>

<h3 id="255">[255] From Packets to Patterns: Interpreting Encrypted Network Traffic as Longitudinal Behavioral Signals</h3>

<h3 id="256">[256] Prosa: Rubric-Based Evaluation of LLMs on Real User Chats in Brazilian Portuguese</h3>

<h3 id="257">[257] AI Alignment via Incentives and Correction</h3>

<h3 id="258">[258] From Cortical Synchronous Rhythm to Brain Inspired Learning Mechanism: An Oscillatory Spiking Neural Network with Time-Delayed Coordination</h3>

<h3 id="263">[263] Class-Aware Adaptive Differential Privacy in Deep Learning for Sensor-Based Fall Detection</h3>

<h3 id="264">[264] GRAVITY: Architecture-Agnostic Structured Anchoring for Long-Horizon Conversational Memory</h3>

<h3 id="266">[266] Probe-Geometry Alignment: Erasing the Cross-Sequence Memorization Signature Below Chance</h3>

<h3 id="267">[267] SplitZip: Ultra Fast Lossless KV Compression for Disaggregated LLM Serving</h3>

<h3 id="268">[268] TCDA: Thread-Constrained Discourse-Aware Modeling for Conversational Sentiment Quadruple Analysis</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了TCDA框架，结合线程约束DAG和话语感知旋转位置嵌入用于对话情感四元组分析。该方法基于线程约束过滤跨线程噪声，通过双流投影和多尺度频率信号对齐多层语义，在两个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="269">[269] SignVerse-2M: A Two-Million-Clip Pose-Native Universe of 25+ Sign Languages</h3>

<h3 id="270">[270] Motion-Aware Caching for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation</h3>

<h3 id="271">[271] FEDIN: Frequency-Enhanced Deep Interest Network for Click-Through Rate Prediction</h3>

<h3 id="272">[272] GEASS: Training-Free Caption Steering for Hallucination Mitigation in Vision-Language Models</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了GEASS模块，一种用于视觉语言模型幻觉缓解的无训练方法。该方法根据置信度和熵减少量门控字幕内容，在POPE和HallusionBench基准上持续优于普通推理和对比解码，仅需额外两次前向传播。

<h3 id="273">[273] Architectural Obsolescence of Unhardened Agentic-AI Runtimes</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文识别了智能体AI运行时中四种动作与审计记录不一致的故障模式，发现现有系统OpenClaw无法检测任何一种故障。作者提出了enclawed-oss框架，通过七种运行时结构实现完美的故障检测准确率。

<h3 id="274">[274] Talk is Cheap, Communication is Hard: Dynamic Grounding Failures and Repair in Multi-Agent Negotiation</h3>

<h3 id="275">[275] The Compliance Gap: Why AI Systems Promise to Follow Process Instructions but Don't</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01771](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01771) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Alignment, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/seanshin0214/bs-bench)
- **Summary**: 本文识别了"合规差距"现象，即AI系统口头同意遵循流程指令但实际行为却绕过指令。作者证明这种差距在RLHF下结构性地不可避免且无法从文本检测，并发布了首个流程合规性基准BS-Bench。

<h3 id="276">[276] Data driven approach for Outdoor Channel Prediction in 5G and Beyond</h3>

<h3 id="277">[277] Khala: Scaling Acoustic Token Language Models Toward High-Fidelity Music Generation</h3>

<h3 id="279">[279] TMD-Bench: A Multi-Level Evaluation Paradigm for Music-Dance Co-Generation</h3>

<h3 id="282">[282] GeoSAE: Geometric Prior-Guided Layer-Wise Sparse Autoencoder Annotation of Brain MRI Foundation Models</h3>

<h3 id="283">[283] RMGAP: Benchmarking the Generalization of Reward Models across Diverse Preferences</h3>

<h3 id="284">[284] Remote Action Generation: Remote Control with Minimal Communication</h3>

<h3 id="285">[285] Repurposing and Evaluating the (In)Feasibility of Dataset Poisoning enabled Watermarking for Contrastive Learning</h3>

<h3 id="286">[286] Disentangled Anatomy-Disease Diffusion (DADD) for Controllable Ulcerative Colitis Progression Synthesis</h3>

<h3 id="287">[287] Spatiotemporal Hidden-State Dynamics as a Signature of Internal Reasoning in Large Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="288">[288] Quality-Aware Exploration Budget Allocation for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="289">[289] ShiftLIF: Efficient Multi-Level Spiking Neurons with Power-of-Two Quantization</h3>

<h3 id="290">[290] Leveraging Data Symmetries to Select an Optimal Subset of Training Data under Label Noise</h3>

<h3 id="291">[291] BadmintonGRF: A Multimodal Dataset and Benchmark for Markerless Ground Reaction Force Estimation in Badminton</h3>

<h3 id="296">[296] RefusalGuard: Geometry-Preserving Fine-Tuning for Safety in LLMs</h3>

<h3 id="297">[297] PepSpecBench: A Unified Evaluation Benchmark for Peptide Tandem Mass Spectrometry Prediction</h3>

<h3 id="298">[298] Phone2Act: A Low-Cost, Hardware-Agnostic Teleoperation System for Scalable VLA Data Collection</h3>

<h3 id="300">[300] Trojan Hippo: Weaponizing Agent Memory for Data Exfiltration</h3>

<h3 id="308">[308] Principles and Guidelines for Randomized Controlled Trials in AI Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02050](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02050) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christopher Kelly, Angelica Chowdhury, Alexandra Campili, Bimpe Ayoola, Devin Barbour, Thomas Chen Dawson, Ze Shen Chin, Rokas Gipiškis
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI Safety, Benchmark
- **Venue**: TASC 2025
- **Summary**: 本文建立了AI评估随机对照试验(RCT)的标准化框架，借鉴软件工程、经济学、临床科学和心理学的实验实践，提出了五项原则并细化为33条指南。该框架将评估中心从模型输出转向人类表现，形式化了AI情境下的因果推断方法。

<h3 id="309">[309] Coopetition-Gym v1: A Formally Grounded Platform for Mixed-Motive Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning under Strategic Coopetition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02063) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vik Pant, Eric Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Reinforcement Learning, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/vikpant/strategic-coopetition)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Coopetition-Gym v1，一个用于混合动机多智能体强化学习的基准平台，包含20个环境并组织为四种机制类别。平台提供可配置的奖励层、博弈论基线和经过历史案例校准的环境，支持连续动作混合动机环境研究。

<h3 id="310">[310] Pair2Score: Pairwise-to-Absolute Transfer for LLM-Based Essay Scoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02069](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02069) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: İbrahim Rıza Hallaç, Hasan Oğul
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Transfer Learning, Text Classification
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Pair2Score，一个两阶段学习框架，将成对比较转化为绝对评分，采用参数高效的LLaMA适配。第一阶段训练方向性孪生排序器，第二阶段通过可配置的迁移策略训练绝对预测器，在自动作文评分任务上取得了性能提升。

<h3 id="311">[311] Cripping AI: Reimagining AI Through Lived Disability Experiences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02080) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinru Tang, Ting-an Lin, Jingjin Li, Shaomei Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Accessibility Technology, Fairness
- **Summary**: 本文基于残障理论提出了'残障化AI'框架，旨在将残障生活经验置于AI研究和开发的中心。该框架不仅追求AI的无障碍性，更致力于揭示和拆除AI设计中的能力主义假设，通过聋人和手语AI、盲人和视觉辅助AI、口吃和语音AI三个案例展示应用。

<h3 id="312">[312] EditPropBench: Measuring Factual Edit Propagation in Scientific Manuscripts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02083](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02083) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Garvin Kruthof
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Text Editing, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文引入EditPropBench基准，用于测量LLM编辑器是否将事实编辑传播到科学手稿中的相关声明。基准提供受控的手稿级评估和句子级依赖监督，实验显示当前最强的LLM编辑系统仍遗漏约30%的必要级联更新。

<h3 id="313">[313] GETA-3DGS: Automatic Joint Structured Pruning and Quantization for 3D Gaussian Splatting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02086](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02086) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Baobing Zhang, Wanxin Sui
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.GR; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Model Compression, 3D Vision, Quantization
- **Summary**: 本文提出了GETA-3DGS，首个端到端自动联合结构化剪枝和量化框架，用于3D高斯泼溅压缩。该方法通过渲染感知显著性和异构属性混合精度方案，在无需场景特定阈值的情况下实现约5倍存储压缩，同时保持渲染质量。

<h3 id="314">[314] STABLEVAL: Disagreement-Aware and Stable Evaluation of AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02122](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02122) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Akash Bonagiri, Gerard Janno Anderias, Saee Patil, Angelina Lai, Devang Borkar, Gezheng Kang, Ishant Gandhi, Setareh Rafatirad, Houman Homayoun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Data Quality
- **Summary**: 本文引入STABLEVAL，一个感知分歧的评估框架，通过建模潜在项目正确性和标注者特定混淆模式来产生稳定的AI系统排名。相比多数投票聚合方法，该方法在标注者异质性和对抗噪声下产生更稳定且统计可靠的系统排名。

<h3 id="315">[315] Context-Aware Wireless Token Communication via Joint Token Masking and Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02123) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junyong Shin, Joohyuk Park, Yongjeong Oh, Jihong Park, Jinho Choi, Yo-Seb Jeon
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, Natural Language Understanding, Tokenization
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种上下文感知的令牌通信框架，利用掩码语言模型作为发射端和接收端之间的共享上下文模型。发射端选择性掩码可推断的令牌，接收端结合信道似然和上下文先验进行令牌检测，在Europarl和WikiText-103数据集上实现了显著性能提升。

<h3 id="316">[316] Boundary Mass and the Soft-to-Hard Limit in Mixture-of-Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02124](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02124) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Reza Rastegar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.PR
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Deep Learning Theory, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文研究了softmax路由混合专家模型在温度趋近零时的奇异性问题，引入边界质量概念来量化路由接口附近的概率质量。在平滑性和横截性假设下，证明了软到硬风险界限和目标函数的Γ收敛性，揭示了零温度极限由路由接口附近的几何层控制。

<h3 id="317">[317] FLoRA: Fusion-Latent for Optical Reconstruction and Flood Area Segmentation via Cross-Modal Multi-Task Distillation Network</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02137) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jagrati Talreja, Tewodros Syum Gebre, Leila Hashemi-Beni
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Segmentation, Remote Sensing, Knowledge Distillation, Disaster Response
- **Summary**: 本文提出了FLoRA，一个跨模态多任务框架，通过融合SAR和光学数据的互补优势，联合重建高保真光学图像和分割洪水区域。该框架使用轻量级光学教师引导SAR表示进入融合潜在空间，在多个数据集上超越了融合基线方法。

<h3 id="318">[318] On the Optimal Sample Complexity of Offline Multi-Armed Bandits with KL Regularization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02141](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02141) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaixuan Ji, Qiwei Di, Heyang Zhao, Qingyue Zhao, Quanquan Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.ST; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Multi-Armed Bandit, Offline RL, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文对KL正则化离线多臂赌博机的样本复杂度进行了精确分析，证明了KL-PCB算法在大正则化和小正则化情况下的匹配上下界。研究结果提供了离线多臂赌博机在KL正则化下几乎完整的样本复杂度刻画。

<h3 id="319">[319] Cross-Polarization Fusion of VV AND VH SAR Observations for Improved Flood Mapping</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02153) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jagrati Talreja, Tewodros Syum Gebre, Leila Hashemi Beni
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Image Segmentation, Disaster Response
- **Venue**: IGARSS 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究了VV和VH SAR观测的交叉极化融合对洪水制图的改进效果，采用深度学习分割框架联合利用两种极化的互补信息。实验结果表明VV-VH融合在植被和异构洪水区域持续优于单极化模型，实现了更准确的洪水边界描绘。

<h3 id="320">[320] Combining Trained Models in Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02159](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02159) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ujjwal Patil, Javad Ghofrani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Transfer Learning, Survey
- **Summary**: 本文对深度强化学习中预训练知识重用的实证研究进行了PRISMA指导的系统综述，分析了15项研究在源目标相似性、模型多样性和比较公平性三个因素上的表现。研究发现正向结果集中在源任务和目标任务共享实质结构的设置中，计算匹配的比较仍然稀缺。

<h3 id="321">[321] DocSync: Agentic Documentation Maintenance via Critic-Guided Reflexion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02163](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02163) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sidhesh Badrinarayan, Adithya Parthasarathy
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, RAG, Software Documentation
- **Summary**: 本文提出DocSync，一个基于智能体工作流的文档维护系统，通过融合抽象语法树(AST)表示和检索增强生成(RAG)来检测和修复代码与文档之间的语义不一致。系统采用基于Reflexion范式的批评引导 refinement 循环，在资源受限的小型语言模型上显著优于传统编码器-解码器基线。

<h3 id="322">[322] Manifold-Aligned Guided Integrated Gradients for Reliable Feature Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02167) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soyeon Kim, Seongwoo Lim, Kyowoon Lee, Jaesik Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/leekwoon/ma-gig/)
- **Summary**: 本文提出MA-GIG方法，通过在预训练变分自编码器的潜在空间中构建归因路径来解决传统积分梯度在噪声梯度区域不可靠的问题。该方法将路径偏向学习的生成流形，减少离流形噪声，在多个数据集和分类器上优于现有基于路径的归因方法。

<h3 id="323">[323] The Causal Description Gap: Information-Theoretic Separations Across Pearl's Hierarchy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02177) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seyed Morteza Emadi
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.IT; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Information Theory
- **Summary**: 本文量化分析了Pearl因果层次中不同层级查询之间的描述长度差距，证明存在二元无环结构因果模型，其观测分布描述长度为常数，而单变量干预答案预言机的描述长度为Θ(n²)。研究揭示了从观测到干预再到反事实的信息论分离现象。

<h3 id="324">[324] RAFNet: Region-Aware Fusion Network for Pansharpening</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02184](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02184) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianing Zhang, Zijian Zhou, Kai Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Image Fusion
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/PatrickNod/RAFNet)
- **Summary**: 本文提出RAFNet网络用于全色锐化，通过离散小波变换和K-means聚类实现区域自适应卷积核构建，并引入基于语义聚类的稀疏注意力机制降低计算冗余。该方法在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有全色锐化方法。

<h3 id="325">[325] When Alignment Isn't Enough: Response-Path Attacks on LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02187](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02187) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingyu Luo, Zihan Zhang, Zesen Liu, Yuchong Xie, Zhixiang Zhang, Dung Hiu Hilton Yeung, Wai Ip Lai, Ping Chen, Ming Wen, Dongdong She
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文揭示了BYOK智能体架构中的安全漏洞：恶意中继可在LLM生成响应后、智能体执行前篡改内容。提出的Relay Tampering Attack在多个基准上达到99.1%攻击成功率，表明仅靠对齐无法防御此类后对齐篡改攻击。

<h3 id="326">[326] Trees and Graphs with Non Log-concave Dominating Set Sequence via AI Tools</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02193](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02193) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alina Du, Steven Heilman, Greta Panova
- **Subjects**: math.CO; cs.AI; cs.DM; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Graph Learning, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文利用AI工具PatternBoost发现了具有非对数凹支配集序列的图和树的新例子，并证明对于任意正整数m，存在一棵树的支配集序列在至少m个索引处非对数凹。研究还表明一大类毛毛虫图具有对数凹支配集序列。

<h3 id="327">[327] MultiSense-Pneumo: A Multimodal Learning Framework for Pneumonia Screening in Resource-Constrained Settings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02207) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dineth Jayakody, Pasindu Thenahandi, Chameli Dommanige
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出MultiSense-Pneumo多模态框架，整合症状描述、咳嗽音频、语音和胸部X光片进行肺炎筛查。系统设计可在资源受限环境下离线运行，适用于社区卫生工作者和农村诊所等场景。

<h3 id="328">[328] The Conversations Beneath the Code: Triadic Data for Long-Horizon Software Engineering Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02244](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02244) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yelin Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文主张下一代软件工程智能体的训练数据应包含"三元数据"：人类-人类对话、人类-AI会话以及跨职能工作的同步捕获。作者提出了四层证据框架来评估此类训练语料库的质量。

<h3 id="329">[329] On the Privacy of LLMs: An Ablation Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02255](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02255) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Karima Makhlouf, Lamiaa Basyoni, Syed Khaderi, Gabriel Marquez, Peter Sotomango, Mahmoud Awawdah, Sami Zhioua
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Privacy, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文对LLM隐私攻击进行了系统消融研究，包括成员推理、属性推理、数据提取和后门攻击。分析揭示了不同攻击类型之间的明显差异，表明LLM系统中的隐私风险高度依赖于上下文和设计选择。

<h3 id="330">[330] Reliability-Oriented Multilingual Orthopedic Diagnosis: A Domain-Adaptive Modeling and a Conceptual Validation Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02266](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02266) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Danish Ali, Li Xiaojian, Sundas Iqbal, Farrukh Zaidi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 本文评估了英语、印地语和旁遮普语临床笔记的多语言骨科诊断，比较了任务对齐的多语言变换器编码器与零样本指令微调LLM。研究发现领域自适应架构显著提升跨语言判别能力，并提出了确定性智能体验证框架。

<h3 id="331">[331] EdgeLPR: On the Deep Neural Network trade-off between Precision and Performance in LiDAR Place Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02275](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02275) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pierpaolo Serio, Hetian Wang, Zixiang Wei, Vincenzo Infantino, Lorenzo Gentilini, Lorenzo Pollini, Valentina Donzella
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Edge Computing, Quantization
- **Venue**: CoDIT 2026
- **Summary**: 本文探索了资源受限平台上的高效激光雷达地点识别，利用鸟瞰图表示实现轻量级图像网络。研究在FP32、FP16和INT8量化下评估架构，揭示了精度、鲁棒性和效率之间的权衡。

<h3 id="332">[332] HELIX: Hybrid Encoding with Learnable Identity and Cross-dimensional Synthesis for Time Series Imputation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02278](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02278) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fengming Zhang, Wenjie Du, Huan Zhang, Ke Yu, Shen Qu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Imputation, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出HELIX时间序列填补方法，为每个特征分配可学习的特征身份嵌入以在网络中保持一致表示。结合混合时间-特征注意力，该方法在5个公开数据集的21个实验设置中超越所有16个基线。

<h3 id="333">[333] Rethinking Electro-Optical Vision Foundation Models for Remote Sensing Retrieval: A Controlled Comparison with Generalist VFM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02283) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyobin Park, Minseok Seo, Dong-Geol Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 本文对遥感图像检索中的电光视觉基础模型与通用视觉基础模型进行了受控比较。结果表明强通用模型与领域特定模型相当甚至更优，且在跨场景评估中表现更稳定，说明EO预训练本身不保证更强的检索表示。

<h3 id="334">[334] When Attention Collapses: Residual Evidence Modeling for Compositional Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02323](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02323) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Niklas Houba
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Compositional Inference, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文识别了注意力机制在加性叠加下的"槽位坍缩"失败模式：多个槽位收敛到同一主导成分。作者提出证据耗尽方法，通过跟踪残差证据防止坍缩，在合成基准和真实音频混合中显著减少坍缩现象。

<h3 id="335">[335] LLM-enabled Social Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02335](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02335) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Önder Gürcan, Moharram Challenger
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Social Simulation
- **Venue**: HHAI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文为LLM驱动的社交智能体建立了概念基线，主张智能体应基于角色定义(通过角色描述操作化)进行接地。作者概述了表示、混合控制和评估的研究方向，认为基于角色的定义是将语言能力转化为社交行为的必要基础。

<h3 id="336">[336] APIOT: Autonomous Vulnerability Management Across Bare-Metal Industrial OT Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02346) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adel ElZemity, Budi Arief, Shujun Li, Calvin Brierley, Yichao Wang, Yuxiang Huang, James Pope, Haoxiang Li, George Oikonomou
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, LLM Agent, Vulnerability Detection
- **Summary**: 本文提出APIOT框架，首次实现裸机工业OT设备的自主攻击与修复循环。在290次实验中达到90%任务成功率，表明攻击者专业知识不再是裸机OT利用的约束条件，防御者必须假设LLM增强的对手能够执行自主发现到修复的完整周期。

<h3 id="337">[337] When Correct Isn't Usable: Improving Structured Output Reliability in Small Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02363](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02363) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cosimo Galeone, Minsu Park, Giuseppe Ettorre, Daniele Ligorio
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Prompt Engineering, Small Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文研究了小型语言模型在结构化输出任务中的可靠性差距，发现简单提示策略虽然任务准确率高但输出格式合规率为零。作者提出AloLab系统提示优化器，无需微调即可显著提升JSON输出准确率，同时保持低延迟。

<h3 id="338">[338] Privacy Preserving Machine Learning Workflow: from Anonymization to Personalized Differential Privacy Budgets in Federated Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02372](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02372) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Judith Sáinz-Pardo Díaz, Álvaro López García
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Differential Privacy, Privacy
- **Venue**: FLICS 2026

<h3 id="374">[374] AI-Generated Smells: An Analysis of Code and Architecture in LLM and Agent-Driven Development</h3>

<h3 id="414">[414] Beyond Scalars: Evaluating and Understanding LLM Reasoning via Geometric Progress and Stability</h3>

<h3 id="415">[415] The Norm-Separation Delay Law of Grokking: A First-Principles Theory of Delayed Generalization</h3>

<h3 id="416">[416] Argumentation for Explainable and Globally Contestable Decision Support with LLMs</h3>

<h3 id="417">[417] Towards Safer Large Reasoning Models by Promoting Safety Decision-Making before Chain-of-Thought Generation</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出基于层级相关性传播的归因引导剪枝方法，用于发现和控制LLM中的行为特定电路，仅需移除极少量神经元即可显著减少有害输出并缓解重复文本生成问题。

<h3 id="505">[505] HiFiNet: Hierarchical Fault Identification in Wireless Sensor Networks via Edge-Based Classification and Graph Aggregation</h3>

<h3 id="506">[506] Re-Key-Free, Risky-Free: Adaptable Model Usage Control</h3>

<h3 id="507">[507] Pistachio: Towards Synthetic, Balanced, and Long-Form Video Anomaly Benchmarks</h3>

<h3 id="508">[508] Intelligent Agents with Emotional Intelligence: Current Trends, Challenges, and Future Prospects</h3>

<h3 id="509">[509] Aligning LLMs with Biomedical Knowledge using Balanced Fine-Tuning</h3>

<h3 id="510">[510] Probabilistic Modeling of Multi-rater Medical Image Segmentation for Diversity and Personalization</h3>

<h3 id="511">[511] 2D-ThermAl: Physics-Informed Framework for Thermal Analysis of Circuits using Generative AI</h3>

<h3 id="512">[512] jina-vlm: Small Multilingual Vision Language Model</h3>

<h3 id="513">[513] InfiniteDiffusion: Bridging Learned Fidelity and Procedural Utility for Open-World Terrain Generation</h3>

<h3 id="514">[514] Consist-Retinex: One-Step Noise-Emphasized Consistency Training Accelerates High-Quality Retinex Enhancement</h3>

<h3 id="515">[515] A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Accountability in Public-Sector AI</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文开发了一个法律可解释性框架，用于公共部门AI系统的问责制。该框架结合了从政策手册提取的结构化本体、规则提取管道和求解器推理层，能够检测法律不一致的解释、突出违反的资格规则，并使自动化决策具有可追溯性和可争议性。

<h3 id="516">[516] How Prompts Move Language Model Behavior: Frames, Salience, and Construal as Semantic Control</h3>

<h3 id="517">[517] Disentangling Fact from Sentiment: A Dynamic Conflict-Consensus Framework for Multimodal Fake News Detection</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了动态冲突-共识框架(DCCF)用于多模态假新闻检测，通过将输入解耦为事实和情感空间，并使用物理启发的特征动力学来放大而非抑制跨模态矛盾。实验表明该方法在三个真实数据集上平均准确率提升3.52%。

<h3 id="518">[518] InstructMoLE: Instruction-Guided Mixture of Low-rank Experts for Multi-Conditional Image Generation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了InstructMoLE框架，用于多条件图像生成的指令引导低秩专家混合。该方法使用从用户指令派生的全局路由信号替代逐标记路由，并引入输出空间正交性损失促进专家功能多样性，在多条件生成基准上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="519">[519] LVLM-Aided Alignment of Task-Specific Vision Models</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

<h3 id="520">[520] The Multi-View Paradigm Shift in MRI Radiomics: Predicting MGMT Methylation in Glioblastoma</h3>

<h3 id="522">[522] OpenAI GPT-5 System Card</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.03267](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03267) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aaditya Singh, Adam Fry, Adam Perelman, Adam Tart, Adi Ganesh, Ahmed El-Kishky, Aidan McLaughlin, Aiden Low, AJ Ostrow, Akhila Ananthram, Akshay Nathan, Alan Luo, Alec Helyar, Aleksander Madry, Aleksandr Efremov, Aleksandra Spyra, Alex Baker-Whitcomb, Alex Beutel, Alex Karpenko, Alex Makelov, Alex Neitz, Alex Wei, Alexandra Barr, Alexandre Kirchmeyer, Alexey Ivanov, Alexi Christakis, Alistair Gillespie, Allison Tam, Ally Bennett, Alvin Wan, Alyssa Huang, Amy McDonald Sandjideh, Amy Yang, Ananya Kumar, Andre Saraiva, Andrea Vallone, Andrei Gheorghe, Andres Garcia Garcia, Andrew Braunstein, Andrew Liu, Andrew Schmidt, Andrey Mereskin, Andrey Mishchenko, Andy Applebaum, Andy Rogerson, Ann Rajan, Annie Wei, Anoop Kotha, Anubha Srivastava, Anushree Agrawal, Arun Vijayvergiya, Ashley Tyra, Ashvin Nair, Avi Nayak, Ben Eggers, Bessie Ji, Beth Hoover, Bill Chen, Blair Chen, Boaz Barak, Borys Minaiev, Botao Hao, Bowen Baker, Brad Lightcap, Brandon McKinzie, Brandon Wang, Brendan Quinn, Brian Fioca, Brian Hsu, Brian Yang, Brian Yu, Brian Zhang, Brittany Brenner, Callie Riggins Zetino, Cameron Raymond, Camillo Lugaresi, Carolina Paz, Cary Hudson, Cedric Whitney, Chak Li, Charles Chen, Charlotte Cole, Chelsea Voss, Chen Ding, Chen Shen, Chengdu Huang, Chris Colby, Chris Hallacy, Chris Koch, Chris Lu, Christina Kaplan, Christina Kim, CJ Minott-Henriques, Cliff Frey, Cody Yu, Coley Czarnecki, Colin Reid, Colin Wei, Cory Decareaux, Cristina Scheau

<h3 id="523">[523] From Laboratory to Real-World Applications: Benchmarking Agentic Code Reasoning at the Repository Level</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.03731](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03731) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jia Li, Yuxin Su, Michael R. Lyu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了RepoReason基准，通过溯因断言验证评估大语言模型的仓库级代码推理能力。该基准使用执行驱动的变异框架和动态程序切片，通过三个正交指标量化推理能力，揭示了前沿模型普遍存在聚合缺陷，其中集成宽度是主要的认知瓶颈。

<h3 id="524">[524] False Friends in the Shell: Unveiling the Emoticon Semantic Confusion in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.07885](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07885) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Weipeng Jiang, Xiaoyu Zhang, Juan Zhai, Shiqing Ma, Chao Shen, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Security, AI Safety, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 本文识别了表情符号语义混淆这一漏洞，即大语言模型误解基于ASCII的表情符号而执行意外甚至破坏性操作。对六个LLM的研究显示平均混淆率超过38%，超过90%的混淆响应产生语法有效但偏离用户意图的静默失败。

<h3 id="525">[525] STAGE: A Full-Screenplay Benchmark for Reasoning over Evolving Storie</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.08510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08510) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiuyu Tian, Zequn Liu, Yiding Li, Fengyi Chen, Zequn Liu, Youyong Kong, Fan Guo, Yuyao Li, Jinjing Shen, Zhijing Xie, Yiyun Luo, Xin Zhang, Yingce Xia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Long Context, Knowledge Graph
- **Summary**: 本文提出了STAGE基准，用于评估模型对完整电影剧本的叙事理解能力，包含知识图谱构建、场景级事件摘要、长上下文剧本问答和角色扮演四个任务。基准提供150部电影的清洗剧本、知识图谱和事件/角色中心标注，支持英语和中文。

<h3 id="526">[526] Can LLMs Compress (and Decompress)? Evaluating Code Understanding and Execution via Invertibility</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.13398](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13398) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nickil Maveli, Antonio Vergari, Shay B. Cohen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.PL
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Nickil21/round-trip-code-compression)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了RoundTripCodeEval基准，通过四种代码执行推理任务评估大语言模型的双向推理一致性。实验表明当前LLM缺乏可靠双向代码推理所需的内部一致性，即使经过监督微调和自反思也无法修复基本的算法理解错误。

<h3 id="527">[527] DuFal: Dual-Frequency-Aware Learning for High-Fidelity Extremely Sparse-view CBCT Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.15416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15416) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Reconstruction, Neural Operator
- **Venue**: TMLR 2025
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DuFal框架，通过双路径架构集成频域和空域处理用于稀疏视图CBCT重建。核心创新是高局部分解傅里叶神经算子，包含全局高频增强和局部高频增强两个分支，在LUNA16和ToothFairy数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="528">[528] Can professional translators identify machine-generated text?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.15828](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15828) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michael Farrell
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Text Detection, Text Generation, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Venue**: EAMT 2026
- **Summary**: 本研究调查了专业翻译人员识别AI生成的意大利语短篇小说的能力。69名翻译人员的实验显示，16.2%成功区分合成文本与人类文本，而近同等数量的人则朝相反方向错误分类。低突发性和叙事矛盾被证明是合成作者身份最可靠的指标。

<h3 id="529">[529] MV-S2V: Multi-View Subject-Consistent Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.17756](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17756) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziyang Song, Xinyu Gong, Bangya Liu, Zelin Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR
- **Tags**: Video Generation, 3D Vision, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了多视角主体到视频生成任务(MV-S2V)，通过多个参考视角合成视频以实现3D级别的主体一致性。作者开发了合成数据构建流程，并引入时移旋转位置编码(TS-RoPE)来区分不同主体和同一主体的不同视角。

<h3 id="530">[530] Attention-Based Neural-Augmented Kalman Filter for Legged Robot State Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.18569](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18569) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Seokju Lee, Kyung-Soo Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于注意力的神经增强卡尔曼滤波器用于足式机器人状态估计，通过注意力机制推断滑移引起的误差并进行补偿。该方法在滑移条件下显著提升了状态估计性能。

<h3 id="531">[531] LLM-VA: Resolving the Jailbreak-Overrefusal Trade-off via Vector Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.19487](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19487) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haonan Zhang, Dongxia Wang, Yi Liu, Kexin Chen, Wenhai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Security
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文发现LLM将回答决策和安全性判断编码为近似正交的方向，导致越狱和过度拒绝之间的权衡。作者提出LLM-VA方法，通过闭式权重更新对齐这些向量，使模型的回答意愿因果依赖于安全性评估。

<h3 id="532">[532] When Iterative RAG Beats Ideal Evidence: A Diagnostic Study in Scientific Multi-hop Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.19827](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19827) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mahdi Astaraki, Mohammad Arshi Saloot, Ali Shiraee Kasmaee, Hamidreza Mahyar, Soheila Samiee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Question Answering, Scientific Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文在科学多跳问答领域进行了首个对照机制级诊断研究，比较迭代RAG与静态RAG的性能。结果表明迭代RAG持续优于理想静态RAG上界，最高提升25.6个百分点，尤其对非推理微调模型效果显著。

<h3 id="533">[533] Cross-Session Decoding of Neural Spiking Data via Task-Conditioned Latent Alignment</h3>

<h3 id="536">[536] Gated Relational Alignment via Confidence-based Distillation for Efficient VLMs</h3>

<h3 id="537">[537] VideoGPA: Distilling Geometry Priors for 3D-Consistent Video Generation</h3>

<h3 id="538">[538] MCP-Atlas: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Tool-Use Competency with Real MCP Servers</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了MCP-Atlas大规模工具使用能力基准测试，包含36个真实MCP服务器和220个工具的1000个任务。评估结果显示顶级模型通过率超过50%，主要失败源于工具使用和任务理解不足。

<h3 id="539">[539] aerial-autonomy-stack -- a Faster-than-real-time, Autopilot-agnostic, ROS2 Framework to Simulate and Deploy Perception-based Drones</h3>

<h3 id="540">[540] STEP: Warm-Started Visuomotor Policies with Spatiotemporal Consistency Prediction</h3>

<h3 id="541">[541] Capture Timing-Attention of Events in Clinical Time Series</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Analysis, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 本文引入了个体级时间变换(LITT)架构，使序列事件能够在虚拟相对时间线上进行临时对齐。该方法在3276名乳腺癌患者的真实纵向EHR数据上验证了其可解释性和有效性，用于预测心脏毒性诱发心脏病的发病时间。

<h3 id="542">[542] Control Reinforcement Learning: Interpretable Token-Level Steering of LLMs via Sparse Autoencoder Features</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文引入控制强化学习(CRL)，训练策略在每个token级别选择SAE特征进行引导。该框架提供可解释的干预日志，在多个基准测试上取得改进，并揭示早期层为语法特征、后期层为语义特征的规律。

<h3 id="543">[543] From SFT to RL: Demystifying the Post-Training Pipeline for LLM-based Vulnerability Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14012](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14012) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 本文首次全面研究了基于LLM的漏洞检测后训练流程，证明策略内RL配合GRPO持续优于SFT和其他方法。研究揭示了拒绝采样比理性化监督更有效、细粒度根因判断比简单正确性奖励更可靠等漏洞检测特定指导原则。

<h3 id="544">[544] CrispEdit: Low-Curvature Projections for Scalable Non-Destructive LLM Editing</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了CrispEdit可扩展二阶编辑算法，将能力保持作为显式约束，通过将编辑更新投影到能力损失景观的低曲率子空间来实现。该方法在标准模型编辑基准上实现高编辑成功率，同时将能力退化保持在平均1%以下。

<h3 id="545">[545] HistCAD: A Constraint-Aware Parametric History-Based CAD Representation, Dataset, and Benchmark with Industrial Complexity</h3>

<h3 id="546">[546] VAUQ: Vision-Aware Uncertainty Quantification for LVLM Self-Evaluation</h3>

<h3 id="547">[547] LittleBit-2: Maximizing the Spectral Energy Gain in Sub-1-Bit LLMs via Latent Geometry Alignment</h3>

<h3 id="548">[548] ContextCov: Deriving and Enforcing Executable Constraints from Agent Instruction Files</h3>

<h3 id="549">[549] Recurrent Graph Neural Networks and Arithmetic Circuits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.05140](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05140) (replaced)

<h3 id="550">[550] The Geometric Inductive Bias of Grokking: Bypassing Phase Transitions via Architectural Topology</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文通过修改架构拓扑来研究Grokking现象，发现球面拓扑约束可将Grokking延迟减少20倍以上，而均匀注意力消融可完全绕过Grokking延迟。研究表明消除记忆化阶段取决于架构先验与任务内在对称性的对齐。

<h3 id="551">[551] Grounding Synthetic Data Generation With Vision and Language Models</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个视觉语言引导的框架，用于遥感领域的可解释合成数据增强和评估。作者构建了ARAS400k数据集，包含10万真实图像和30万合成图像，实验表明混合真实和合成数据的训练效果持续优于纯真实数据基线。

<h3 id="552">[552] FastDSAC: Unlocking the Potential of Maximum Entropy RL in High-Dimensional Humanoid Control</h3>

<h3 id="553">[553] Visualizing Critic Match Loss Landscapes for Interpretation of Online Reinforcement Learning Control Algorithms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.14535](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14535) (replaced)

<h3 id="554">[554] DeepStage: Learning Autonomous Defense Policies Against Multi-Stage APT Campaigns</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.16969](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16969) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 本文提出了DeepStage框架，一种用于对抗高级持续性威胁(APT)的深度强化学习防御方法。该方法将企业环境建模为POMDP，使用图神经网络编码器和LSTM阶段估计器推断攻击者阶段，在CALDERA驱动的APT测试中实现了0.887的F1分数和84.7%的缓解成功率。

<h3 id="555">[555] A Synthesizable RTL Implementation of Predictive Coding Networks</h3>

<h3 id="556">[556] Retrieval-Augmented LLMs for Security Incident Analysis</h3>

<h3 id="557">[557] Design-OS: A Specification-Driven Framework for Engineering System Design with a Control-Systems Design Case</h3>

<h3 id="558">[558] PiCSRL: Physics-Informed Contextual Spectral Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="559">[559] GISclaw: A Comprehensive Open-Source LLM Agent System for Realistic Multi-Step Geospatial Analysis</h3>

<h3 id="560">[560] Omni-NegCLIP: Enhancing CLIP with Front-Layer Contrastive Fine-Tuning for Comprehensive Negation Understanding</h3>

- **Summary**: 该研究提出一种基于梯度下降和物理约束的吸引子模糊认知图模型，采用残差记忆、时间反向传播和递归实现的固定点锚点，结合牛顿法和自适应梯度下降优化权重更新。

<h3 id="599">[599] Models Recall What They Violate: Constraint Adherence in Multi-Turn LLM Ideation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.28031](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28031) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Garvin Kruthof
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该研究提出DriftBench基准测试，发现LLM在多轮科学构思中迭代压力会增加结构复杂性并降低约束遵循度，模型能够准确复述约束却同时违反约束，结构化检查点可部分缓解但无法消除这一分离现象。

<h3 id="600">[600] Beyond SFT-to-RL: Pre-alignment via Black-Box On-Policy Distillation for Multimodal RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.28123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28123) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sudong Wang, Weiquan Huang, Xiaomin Yu, Zuhao Yang, Hehai Lin, Keming Wu, Chaojun Xiao, Chen Chen, Wenxuan Wang, Beier Zhu, Yunjian Zhang, Chengwei Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Knowledge Distillation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/XIAO4579/PRISM)
- **Summary**: 该研究提出PRISM三阶段流水线，通过黑盒策略蒸馏在SFT和强化学习之间插入分布对齐阶段，利用混合专家判别器提供感知和推理的解耦校正信号，有效缓解多模态推理中的分布漂移问题。

<h3 id="601">[601] REALM: An RGB and Event Aligned Latent Manifold for Cross-Modal Perception</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00271](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00271) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vincenzo Polizzi, David B. Lindell, Jonathan Kelly
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Vision Foundation Model, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 该研究提出REALM跨模态框架，通过低秩适应将事件相机数据映射到预训练RGB基础模型的潜在空间，实现对深度估计、语义分割等下游任务的零样本迁移，在宽基线特征匹配上达到最优性能。

<h3 id="602">[602] Social Bias in LLM-Generated Code: Benchmark and Mitigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00382) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fazle Rabbi, Lin Ling, Song Wang, Jinqiu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Fairness, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该研究使用SocialBias-Bench基准全面评估LLM生成代码中的社会偏见，发现所有模型存在严重偏见，标准提示干预反而放大偏见，提出的公平性监控代理可将偏见降低65.1%同时提升功能正确性。

<h3 id="603">[603] RadLite: Multi-Task LoRA Fine-Tuning of Small Language Models for CPU-Deployable Radiology AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00421) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pankaj Gupta, Kartik Bose
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Small Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RadioX-Labs/RadLite)
- **Summary**: 该研究通过LoRA微调3-4B参数的小语言模型实现多任务放射学AI，在9项任务上显著超越零样本基线，模型可量化至CPU部署，证明了小模型在资源受限临床环境中的实用性。

<h3 id="604">[604] Silicon Showdown: Performance, Efficiency, and Ecosystem Barriers in Consumer-Grade LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00519](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00519) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Abdurrahman Javat, Allan Kazakov
- **Subjects**: cs.PF; cs.AI; cs.AR
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, LLM Deployment, Energy Efficiency
- **Summary**: 该研究系统分析了Nvidia和Apple Silicon生态系统在消费级LLM推理中的性能、效率和生态障碍，发现Nvidia的NVFP4量化提供1.6倍吞吐量优势但面临VRAM墙，而Apple统一内存架构在能效上具有23倍优势。
